## 3. Resumable extraction script for remaining pages (`scripts/extract_remaining.py`)

This uses **PyMuPDF** (`fitz`), skips pages that already have an `.md` file, applies the *same* cleaning rules, and writes output in the same format.

```python
# scripts/extract_remaining.py
"""
Extract remaining PDF pages to ./md/ using PyMuPDF.
Resumable: skips any page that already has a matching markdown file.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import re
import sys
from pathlib import Path

import fitz  # PyMuPDF

# Reuse cleaning logic
sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent))
from clean_md import clean_text, build_frontmatter  # noqa: E402

PDF_PATH = "service_manual.pdf"   # <-- change to your actual PDF path
MD_DIR = Path("./md")
MD_DIR.mkdir(exist_ok=True)

# Filename convention: page_001.md (zero-padded to 3 digits)
def md_path_for_page(page: int) -> Path:
    return MD_DIR / f"page_{page:03d}.md"

def extract_heading(text: str) -> str:
    """Crude heading guess: first meaningful line, or first line that looks like a heading."""
    for line in text.splitlines():
        s = line.strip()
        if len(s) > 3 and not s.isdigit():
            return s[:80]
    return "Untitled"

def main() -> None:
    doc = fitz.open(PDF_PATH)
    total = doc.page_count
    print(f"PDF has {total} pages.")

    existing = {int(re.search(r"(\d+)", f.stem).group(1)) for f in MD_DIR.glob("page_*.md") if re.search(r"\d", f.stem)}
    print(f"Already extracted: {sorted(existing)}")

    for i in range(total):
        pdf_page_number = i + 1  # 1-based
        if pdf_page_number in existing:
            continue

        page = doc[i]
        raw_text = page.get_text("text")  # or "blocks" for table-aware extraction
        if not raw_text.strip():
            print(f"Page {pdf_page_number}: no text (possible image-only page). Skipping.")
            continue

        cleaned = clean_text(raw_text)
        heading = extract_heading(cleaned)
        frontmatter = build_frontmatter({}, page=pdf_page_number, heading=heading)
        out = frontmatter + cleaned

        out_path = md_path_for_page(pdf_page_number)
        out_path.write_text(out, encoding="utf-8")
        print(f"Extracted page {pdf_page_number} -> {out_path.name}")

    print("Done.")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

**Requirements:** `pip install pymupdf`

Run:
```bash
python scripts/extract_remaining.py
```

## Step 1: Inspection Script

Save this as `inspect_md.py` in your project root and run it:

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
inspect_md.py
Inspect existing markdown files in ./md/ and report:
- naming convention
- format (frontmatter, headings, page markers)
- cleaning problems (headers/footers, hyphenation, broken tables, etc.)
"""
from pathlib import Path
import re
import json
from collections import Counter

MD_DIR = Path("./md")

def load_text(path: Path) -> str:
    return path.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")

def inspect():
    if not MD_DIR.exists():
        print(f"ERROR: {MD_DIR} not found. Create it or check the path.")
        return

    files = sorted(MD_DIR.glob("*.md"))
    if not files:
        print(f"No .md files found in {MD_DIR}")
        return

    print(f"=== INSPECTION OF {MD_DIR} ===")
    print(f"Total markdown files: {len(files)}\n")

    # ---------- Naming ----------
    names = [f.name for f in files]
    print("--- Naming ---")
    print("First 10 names:", names[:10])
    print("Last 10 names:", names[-10:])

    pat_counter = Counter()
    for n in names:
        if re.fullmatch(r"page_\d+\.md", n):
            pat_counter["page_N.md"] += 1
        elif re.fullmatch(r"\d+\.md", n):
            pat_counter["N.md"] += 1
        elif re.fullmatch(r"p\d+\.md", n, re.I):
            pat_counter["pN.md"] += 1
        else:
            pat_counter["other"] += 1
    print("Naming pattern distribution:", dict(pat_counter))

    # Extract page numbers from names if possible
    pages_found = []
    for n in names:
        m = re.search(r"(\d+)", n)
        if m:
            pages_found.append(int(m.group(1)))
    if pages_found:
        print(f"Page range in names: {min(pages_found)} - {max(pages_found)}")
        missing = [p for p in range(min(pages_found), max(pages_found)+1) if p not in pages_found]
        if missing:
            print(f"Missing pages in sequence (first 20): {missing[:20]}")

    # ---------- Content ----------
    print("\n--- Content Analysis ---")
    total_chars = 0
    empty_files = []
    frontmatter_count = 0
    heading_levels = Counter()
    page_marker_count = 0
    issues = Counter()
    examples = {}

    for f in files:
        text = load_text(f)
        total_chars += len(text)
        if not text.strip():
            empty_files.append(f.name)
            continue

        # Frontmatter?
        if text.lstrip().startswith("---"):
            frontmatter_count += 1

        # Headings
        for m in re.finditer(r"^(#{1,6})\s+(.+)$", text, re.M):
            heading_levels[len(m.group(1))] += 1

        # Page markers
        if re.search(r"(?:^|\n)#+\s*Page\s*\d+", text, re.I):
            page_marker_count += 1
        if re.search(r"\bPage\s+\d+\b", text, re.I):
            issues["explicit_page_text_in_body"] += 1

        # Common cleaning problems
        if re.search(r"-\n", text):
            issues["hyphenation_line_break"] += 1
        if "\f" in text:
            issues["form_feed_char"] += 1
        if re.search(r" {3,}", text):
            issues["multiple_spaces"] += 1
        if re.search(r"(?m)^\s*(?:header|footer|page\s+\d+\s+of\s+\d+)\s*$", text, re.I):
            issues["header_footer_line"] += 1
        if re.search(r"\|\s*-+\s*\|", text):
            issues["possible_broken_table"] += 1

        # Store a short example for the first file of each naming pattern
        key = "page_N.md" if re.fullmatch(r"page_\d+\.md", f.name) else "other"
        if key not in examples:
            examples[key] = (f.name, text[:1500])

    print(f"Total characters: {total_chars:,}")
    print(f"Files with YAML frontmatter: {frontmatter_count}")
    print(f"Files with page-marker heading: {page_marker_count}")
    print(f"Empty files: {empty_files[:10]}{'...' if len(empty_files)>10 else ''}")
    print(f"Heading level distribution: {dict(heading_levels)}")
    print(f"Issue counts (across all files): {dict(issues)}")

    # ---------- Samples ----------
    print("\n--- Sample Content (first file of each pattern) ---")
    for key, (name, snippet) in examples.items():
        print(f"\n>>> {name} <<<")
        print(snippet)
        print("...")

    # ---------- Heuristic recommendation ----------
    print("\n--- Heuristic Recommendation ---")
    if issues["hyphenation_line_break"]:
        print("- Hyphenation detected: enable de-hyphenation in cleaner.")
    if issues["header_footer_line"]:
        print("- Header/footer lines detected: add regex stripping.")
    if frontmatter_count == 0:
        print("- No frontmatter detected: consider adding page metadata as YAML frontmatter.")
    if heading_levels and 1 not in heading_levels:
        print("- No H1 headings detected: section structure may be weak.")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    inspect()
```

### Step 1a: Inspection script (`scripts/inspect_md.py`)

Run this from the project root. It reports naming, format, metadata preservation, and common cleaning problems.

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Inspect ./md/ files: naming, format, metadata, and cleaning issues."""
from pathlib import Path
import re
import json
from collections import Counter

MD_DIR = Path("./md")

def inspect_md():
    if not MD_DIR.exists():
        print(f"ERROR: {MD_DIR} not found.")
        return

    files = sorted(MD_DIR.glob("*.md"))
    report = {
        "directory": str(MD_DIR.resolve()),
        "total_files": len(files),
        "naming_examples": [f.name for f in files[:10]],
        "naming_patterns": Counter(),
        "has_frontmatter": 0,
        "has_page_marker": 0,
        "has_heading": 0,
        "avg_lines": 0,
        "issues": [],
        "samples": [],
    }

    total_lines = 0
    for f in files:
        text = f.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
        lines = text.splitlines()
        total_lines += len(lines)

        # Naming pattern (rough)
        stem = f.stem
        if re.fullmatch(r"page_\d+", stem):
            report["naming_patterns"]["page_N"] += 1
        elif re.fullmatch(r"\d+", stem):
            report["naming_patterns"]["N"] += 1
        elif re.fullmatch(r"p\d+", stem):
            report["naming_patterns"]["pN"] += 1
        else:
            report["naming_patterns"]["other"] += 1

        # Metadata checks
        if text.lstrip().startswith("---"):
            report["has_frontmatter"] += 1
        if re.search(r"(?i)page\s+\d+", text):
            report["has_page_marker"] += 1
        if re.search(r"^#\s+", text, re.M):
            report["has_heading"] += 1

        # Cleaning-problem heuristics
        if "\x00" in text:
            report["issues"].append({"file": f.name, "issue": "null bytes present"})
        if re.search(r"-\n[a-z]", text):
            report["issues"].append({"file": f.name, "issue": "possible hyphenation line-break"})
        if re.search(r"(?i)^\s*(header|footer|page \d+ of \d+)\s*$", text, re.M):
            report["issues"].append({"file": f.name, "issue": "possible header/footer line"})
        if re.search(r"\f", text):
            report["issues"].append({"file": f.name, "issue": "form feed characters present"})

        # Sample first 5 files in detail
        if len(report["samples"]) < 5:
            report["samples"].append({
                "file": f.name,
                "size_bytes": f.stat().st_size,
                "line_count": len(lines),
                "first_line": lines[0] if lines else "",
                "second_line": lines[1] if len(lines) > 1 else "",
                "last_line": lines[-1] if lines else "",
            })

    report["avg_lines"] = round(total_lines / len(files), 1) if files else 0

    print(json.dumps(report, indent=2))

if __name__ == "__main__":
    inspect_md()
```

**What to look for in the output**
| Check | Good sign | Problem sign |
|-------|-----------|--------------|
| Naming | `page_001.md`, `page_002.md` | Mixed `1.md`, `p1.md`, `page1.md` |
| Frontmatter | YAML block with `page:` and `section:` | None |
| Page marker | `--- Page 42 ---` or `page: 42` | Missing page numbers |
| Headings | `# 10. Differential Assembly` | No headings |
| Hyphenation | None | `step-\nplate` |
| Headers/footers | None | Repeated `Predii Service Manual` / page numbers |
| Null bytes / form feeds | None | Present |

### Step 1b: Reusable cleaning script (`scripts/clean_md.py`)

Run this **after** inspection. It applies the same rules to every file so your existing pages and the newly extracted pages stay consistent.

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Clean markdown files in ./md/ with reusable rules."""
from pathlib import Path
import re
import sys

MD_DIR = Path("./md")

# Customize these regexes to match your manual's actual header/footer text
HEADER_FOOTER_PATTERNS = [
    re.compile(r"^\s*Predii.*Service Manual\s*$", re.I),
    re.compile(r"^\s*Page\s+\d+\s+of\s+\d+\s*$", re.I),
    re.compile(r"^\s*\d+\s*$"),  # lone page number
]

def clean_text(text: str) -> str:
    # 1. Remove null bytes and form feeds
    text = text.replace("\x00", "").replace("\f", "\n")

    # 2. Fix hyphenation across line breaks (conservative: only lowercase after hyphen)
    text = re.sub(r"(\w)-\n([a-z])", r"\1 \2", text)

    # 3. Collapse 3+ blank lines into 2
    text = re.sub(r"\n{3,}", "\n\n", text)

    # 4. Strip trailing whitespace per line
    text = "\n".join(line.rstrip() for line in text.splitlines())

    # 5. Remove detected header/footer lines
    for pat in HEADER_FOOTER_PATTERNS:
        text = pat.sub("", text)

    # 6. Collapse blank lines created by removal
    text = re.sub(r"\n{3,}", "\n\n", text)

    return text.strip() + "\n"

def main():
    if not MD_DIR.exists():
        print(f"ERROR: {MD_DIR} not found.")
        sys.exit(1)

    for f in sorted(MD_DIR.glob("*.md")):
        original = f.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
        cleaned = clean_text(original)
        if cleaned != original:
            f.write_text(cleaned, encoding="utf-8")
            print(f"cleaned: {f.name}")
        else:
            print(f"ok:      {f.name}")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

### 1. Inspection script: `inspect_md.py`

Save this as `inspect_md.py` and run `python inspect_md.py`.

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
inspect_md.py

Inspects ./md/*.md files for:
- Naming convention
- Page number / heading preservation
- YAML frontmatter
- Repeated headers/footers across pages
- Broken tables (markdown pipe alignment)
- Hyphenation at line breaks
- Figure/tool labels and watermarks common in service manuals
"""
from pathlib import Path
import re
import json
from collections import Counter

MD_DIR = Path("./md")
REPORT_PATH = Path("inspection_report.json")

# Patterns typical of vehicle service manuals
PATTERNS = {
    "page_number_standalone": re.compile(r"^\s*\d{1,4}\s*$", re.MULTILINE),
    "figure_label": re.compile(r"\bN\d{5,7}\b"),          # e.g. N0013866
    "tool_number": re.compile(r"\b\d{3}-\d{3}\b"),        # e.g. 205-378
    "torque_value": re.compile(r"\b\d+\s*(?:Nm|lb-?ft|ft-?lb|kg-?m)\b", re.IGNORECASE),
    "watermark_repair4less": re.compile(r"repair4less", re.IGNORECASE),
    "hyphen_line_break": re.compile(r"-\s*\n\s*\w"),      # word split across lines
    "table_row": re.compile(r"^\s*\|.*\|\s*$", re.MULTILINE),
    "step_number": re.compile(r"^\s*\d+\.\s", re.MULTILINE),
    "heading_md": re.compile(r"^#{1,6}\s+.+", re.MULTILINE),
}

def analyze_file(path: Path) -> dict:
    text = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
    lines = text.splitlines()

    findings = {
        "name": path.name,
        "size_bytes": path.stat().st_size,
        "n_lines": len(lines),
        "n_chars": len(text),
        "first_line": lines[0].strip() if lines else "",
        "last_line": lines[-1].strip() if lines else "",
        "has_yaml_frontmatter": text.startswith("---\n"),
        "heading_count": len(PATTERNS["heading_md"].findall(text)),
        "step_count": len(PATTERNS["step_number"].findall(text)),
        "figure_labels": PATTERNS["figure_label"].findall(text)[:10],
        "tool_numbers": PATTERNS["tool_number"].findall(text)[:10],
        "torque_values": PATTERNS["torque_value"].findall(text)[:10],
        "has_watermark": bool(PATTERNS["watermark_repair4less"].search(text)),
        "hyphen_line_breaks": len(PATTERNS["hyphen_line_break"].findall(text)),
        "table_rows": len(PATTERNS["table_row"].findall(text)),
        "standalone_page_numbers": len(PATTERNS["page_number_standalone"].findall(text)),
    }
    return findings

def find_repeated_lines(files: list[Path], min_occurrences: int = 2) -> list[tuple[str, int]]:
    """Find lines that appear in many files (likely headers/footers)."""
    line_counter: Counter[str] = Counter()
    file_lines: dict[str, set[str]] = {}

    for path in files:
        text = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
        lines = {ln.strip() for ln in text.splitlines() if ln.strip()}
        file_lines[path.name] = lines
        for ln in lines:
            line_counter[ln] += 1

    repeated = [(ln, cnt) for ln, cnt in line_counter.items() if cnt >= min_occurrences]
    repeated.sort(key=lambda x: -x[1])
    return repeated[:30]

def main():
    if not MD_DIR.exists():
        print(f"ERROR: {MD_DIR} not found. Create it or run from the project root.")
        return

    files = sorted(MD_DIR.glob("*.md"))
    if not files:
        print(f"No markdown files found in {MD_DIR}")
        return

    print(f"Found {len(files)} markdown files in {MD_DIR}\n")

    per_file = [analyze_file(f) for f in files]

    # Cross-file repeated lines (headers/footers)
    repeated = find_repeated_lines(files)

    report = {
        "n_files": len(files),
        "file_names": [f.name for f in files],
        "per_file": per_file,
        "repeated_lines_across_files": repeated,
    }

    # Console summary
    print("=== Per-file summary ===")
    for p in per_file:
        print(f"\n{p['name']}")
        print(f"  size={p['size_bytes']}B  lines={p['n_lines']}  chars={p['n_chars']}")
        print(f"  yaml_frontmatter={p['has_yaml_frontmatter']}  headings={p['heading_count']}  steps={p['step_count']}")
        print(f"  figure_labels={p['figure_labels']}")
        print(f"  tool_numbers={p['tool_numbers']}")
        print(f"  torque_values={p['torque_values']}")
        print(f"  watermark={p['has_watermark']}  hyphen_breaks={p['hyphen_line_breaks']}  table_rows={p['table_rows']}  standalone_pages={p['standalone_page_numbers']}")
        print(f"  first: {p['first_line'][:80]}")
        print(f"  last:  {p['last_line'][:80]}")

    print("\n=== Repeated lines across files (likely headers/footers) ===")
    for ln, cnt in repeated:
        print(f"  [{cnt}x] {ln[:100]}")

    REPORT_PATH.write_text(json.dumps(report, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
    print(f"\nFull report written to {REPORT_PATH}")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

Below is a **reusable inspection + cleaning script** you can run against your existing `./md/` folder. It audits naming, page markers, headings, and common PDF-extraction artifacts (headers/footers, hyphenation, broken tables), then applies deterministic cleaning rules and writes a JSON audit report.

Run it, then paste the `audit_report.json` output (or a summary) here so I can confirm the format before building Step 2 (the resumable PyMuPDF extractor for the remaining pages).

---

### `audit_and_clean_md.py`

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
Audit and clean existing markdown files in ./md/.
- Reports naming, page markers, headings, and cleaning problems.
- Applies reusable cleaning rules (headers/footers, hyphenation, whitespace).
- Writes cleaned files back to ./md/ (or --out-dir) and an audit report JSON.
"""

import argparse
import json
import re
from collections import Counter, defaultdict
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Dict, List, Tuple

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Configuration
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
MD_DIR = Path("./md")
OUT_DIR = Path("./md_cleaned")
REPORT_PATH = Path("audit_report.json")

# Patterns
PAGE_MARKER_RE = re.compile(r"(?:^|\n)\s*(?:#+\s*)?page\s*(\d+)\s*(?:\n|$)", re.IGNORECASE)
HEADING_RE = re.compile(r"^\s{0,3}#{1,6}\s+\S", re.MULTILINE)
STANDALONE_NUMBER_RE = re.compile(r"^\s*\d{1,4}\s*$", re.MULTILINE)
HYPHEN_BREAK_RE = re.compile(r"(\w+)-\n(\w+)")
MULTISPACE_RE = re.compile(r"[ \t]{2,}")
TRAILING_WS_RE = re.compile(r"[ \t]+$", re.MULTILINE)
MULTIPLE_NEWLINES_RE = re.compile(r"\n{3,}")


def load_files(md_dir: Path) -> List[Path]:
    return sorted(md_dir.glob("*.md"))


def detect_naming_pattern(files: List[Path]) -> Counter:
    """Bucket filenames by a simple pattern (e.g. page_42, p42, 042)."""
    patterns = Counter()
    for f in files:
        stem = f.stem.lower()
        if re.search(r"page", stem):
            patterns["page_*"] += 1
        elif re.search(r"^p\d+", stem):
            patterns["p*"] += 1
        elif re.search(r"^\d+", stem):
            patterns["numeric_only"] += 1
        else:
            patterns["other"] += 1
    return patterns


def find_repeated_lines(files_text: Dict[Path, str], min_occurrences: int = 2) -> List[str]:
    """Find lines that appear in many files (likely running headers/footers)."""
    line_counts = Counter()
    file_presence = defaultdict(set)

    for path, text in files_text.items():
        for line in text.splitlines():
            stripped = line.strip()
            if not stripped:
                continue
            file_presence[stripped].add(path.name)

    repeated = [
        line for line, files in file_presence.items()
        if len(files) >= min_occurrences and len(line) < 100
    ]
    return sorted(repeated, key=lambda x: -len(file_presence[x]))


def audit_file(path: Path, text: str, repeated_lines: set) -> Dict:
    issues = []

    # Page marker
    page_match = PAGE_MARKER_RE.search(text)
    page_number = int(page_match.group(1)) if page_match else None
    if not page_number:
        issues.append("missing_page_marker")

    # Headings
    has_headings = bool(HEADING_RE.search(text))
    if not has_headings:
        issues.append("no_headings")

    # Header/footer artifacts (lines that are repeated across files)
    lines = text.splitlines()
    artifact_lines = [ln for ln in lines if ln.strip() in repeated_lines]
    if artifact_lines:
        issues.append(f"repeated_artifact_lines:{len(artifact_lines)}")

    # Standalone page numbers (orphaned digits)
    orphan_numbers = STANDALONE_NUMBER_RE.findall(text)
    if orphan_numbers:
        issues.append(f"standalone_numbers:{len(orphan_numbers)}")

    # Hyphenation breaks across lines
    hyphen_breaks = HYPHEN_BREAK_RE.findall(text)
    if hyphen_breaks:
        issues.append(f"hyphenation_breaks:{len(hyphen_breaks)}")

    # Broken table heuristic: rows starting/ending with pipe but inconsistent cell counts
    table_lines = [ln for ln in lines if ln.strip().startswith("|") and ln.strip().endswith("|")]
    if table_lines:
        cell_counts = [ln.count("|") for ln in table_lines]
        if len(set(cell_counts)) > 1:
            issues.append("inconsistent_table_columns")

    return {
        "file": path.name,
        "page": page_number,
        "chars": len(text),
        "lines": len(lines),
        "has_headings": has_headings,
        "issues": issues,
    }


def clean_text(text: str, repeated_lines: set) -> str:
    """Apply deterministic cleaning rules."""
    t = text

    # 1. Remove repeated header/footer lines entirely
    lines = t.splitlines()
    cleaned_lines = [ln for ln in lines if ln.strip() not in repeated_lines]
    t = "\n".join(cleaned_lines)

    # 2. Remove standalone page-number lines (orphaned digits)
    t = STANDALONE_NUMBER_RE.sub("", t)

    # 3. Fix hyphenation breaks: "trans-\nmission" -> "transmission"
    t = HYPHEN_BREAK_RE.sub(r"\1\2", t)

    # 4. Normalize whitespace
    t = TRAILING_WS_RE.sub("", t)
    t = MULTISPACE_RE.sub(" ", t)
    t = MULTIPLE_NEWLINES_RE.sub("\n\n", t)

    # 5. Ensure single trailing newline
    t = t.strip() + "\n"
    return t


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Audit and clean extracted markdown files.")
    parser.add_argument("--md-dir", default=str(MD_DIR), help="Input markdown directory")
    parser.add_argument("--out-dir", default=str(OUT_DIR), help="Output cleaned directory")
    parser.add_argument("--report", default=str(REPORT_PATH), help="Audit report JSON path")
    parser.add_argument("--dry-run", action="store_true", help="Do not write cleaned files")
    args = parser.parse_args()

    md_dir = Path(args.md_dir)
    out_dir = Path(args.out_dir)
    if not args.dry_run:
        out_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    files = load_files(md_dir)
    if not files:
        print(f"No markdown files found in {md_dir}")
        return

    # Load all texts
    files_text = {f: f.read_text(encoding="utf-8") for f in files}

    # Naming patterns
    naming = detect_naming_pattern(files)

    # Repeated lines across all files (candidate headers/footers)
    repeated = find_repeated_lines(files_text, min_occurrences=max(2, len(files) // 2))
    repeated_set = set(repeated)

    # Per-file audit
    file_audits = []
    for path, text in files_text.items():
        file_audits.append(audit_file(path, text, repeated_set))

    # Summary
    total = len(files)
    with_pages = sum(1 for a in file_audits if a["page"] is not None)
    with_headings = sum(1 for a in file_audits if a["has_headings"])
    with_issues = sum(1 for a in file_audits if a["issues"])

    issue_counter = Counter()
    for a in file_audits:
        for iss in a["issues"]:
            key = iss.split(":")[0]
            issue_counter[key] += 1

    report = {
        "input_dir": str(md_dir),
        "output_dir": str(out_dir) if not args.dry_run else None,
        "total_files": total,
        "naming_patterns": dict(naming),
        "files_with_page_markers": with_pages,
        "files_with_headings": with_headings,
        "files_with_issues": with_issues,
        "repeated_header_footer_candidates": repeated[:20],
        "issue_summary": dict(issue_counter),
        "file_details": file_audits,
    }

    # Write cleaned files
    if not args.dry_run:
        for path, text in files_text.items():
            cleaned = clean_text(text, repeated_set)
            (out_dir / path.name).write_text(cleaned, encoding="utf-8")

    # Write report
    Path(args.report).write_text(json.dumps(report, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")

    # Console summary
    print(f"Audited {total} files in {md_dir}")
    print(f"Naming patterns: {dict(naming)}")
    print(f"With page markers: {with_pages}/{total}")
    print(f"With headings: {with_headings}/{total}")
    print(f"With issues: {with_issues}/{total}")
    print(f"Issue summary: {dict(issue_counter)}")
    print(f"Repeated header/footer candidates: {repeated[:10]}")
    print(f"Report written to {args.report}")
    if not args.dry_run:
        print(f"Cleaned files written to {out_dir}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

#### `scripts/clean_md.py` — reusable cleaning for existing + new files

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
Reusable markdown cleaner for service-manual pages.
Applies the same rules to already-extracted files and newly extracted pages.
"""
from __future__ import annotations
import re
from pathlib import Path

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# CONFIGURATION — adjust these after inspecting your real files
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
MD_DIR = Path("./md")
PAGE_MARKER_RE = re.compile(r"^#+\s*Page\s+(\d+)\s*$", re.IGNORECASE)  # e.g. "# Page 42"
FOOTER_PATTERNS = [
    re.compile(r"repair4less", re.IGNORECASE),
    re.compile(r"^\s*\d+\s*$"),  # lone page-number footers
    re.compile(r"^\s*Service Manual\s*$", re.IGNORECASE),
]
HEADER_PATTERNS = [
    re.compile(r"^\s*Chapter\s+\d+", re.IGNORECASE),
]

def fix_hyphenation(text: str) -> str:
    """Join words broken across lines by hyphen + newline."""
    return re.sub(r"(\w)-\n(\w)", r"\1\2", text)

def remove_boilerplate(lines: list[str]) -> list[str]:
    """Drop repeated header/footer lines while preserving real content."""
    cleaned = []
    for line in lines:
        stripped = line.strip()
        if any(p.search(stripped) for p in FOOTER_PATTERNS + HEADER_PATTERNS):
            # Keep the line if it also looks like a real heading (defensive)
            if PAGE_MARKER_RE.match(stripped):
                cleaned.append(line)
            continue
        cleaned.append(line)
    return cleaned

def clean_markdown(text: str) -> str:
    lines = text.splitlines()
    lines = remove_boilerplate(lines)
    text = "\n".join(lines)
    text = fix_hyphenation(text)
    # Collapse 3+ blank lines to 2
    text = re.sub(r"\n{3,}", "\n\n", text)
    return text.strip() + "\n"

def clean_all(md_dir: Path = MD_DIR) -> None:
    for md_file in sorted(md_dir.glob("*.md")):
        raw = md_file.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        cleaned = clean_markdown(raw)
        md_file.write_text(cleaned, encoding="utf-8")
        print(f"cleaned: {md_file.name}")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    clean_all()
```

#### `scripts/extract_remaining.py` — PyMuPDF extraction for remaining pages only

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
Extract remaining PDF pages to ./md/ using the same format/cleaning as existing files.
Resumable: skips pages that already have an .md file.
"""
from __future__ import annotations
import fitz  # PyMuPDF
import re
from pathlib import Path
import sys

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# CONFIGURATION — set these after inspecting your files
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
PDF_PATH = Path("./service_manual.pdf")   # <-- update
MD_DIR = Path("./md")
START_PAGE = 1        # 1-based; first page to consider
END_PAGE = None       # None = end of document
PAGE_TEMPLATE = "page_{:03d}.md"          # <-- match your existing naming

# Reuse the same cleaning rules
sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent))
from clean_md import clean_markdown  # noqa: E402

def page_has_md(md_dir: Path, pdf_page_index: int) -> bool:
    """Check if an md file already exists for this PDF page."""
    name = PAGE_TEMPLATE.format(pdf_page_index + 1)  # convert to 1-based
    return (md_dir / name).exists()

def extract_page(doc: fitz.Document, pdf_page_index: int) -> str:
    page = doc[pdf_page_index]
    text = page.get_text("text")
    # Insert a page marker so downstream chunking can use it
    return f"# Page {pdf_page_index + 1}\n\n{text}\n"

def extract_remaining(pdf_path: Path, md_dir: Path, start: int, end: int | None) -> None:
    md_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    doc = fitz.open(str(pdf_path))
    total = doc.page_count
    end = end or total

    skipped, extracted = 0, 0
    for i in range(start - 1, min(end, total)):
        if page_has_md(md_dir, i):
            skipped += 1
            continue
        raw_md = extract_page(doc, i)
        cleaned_md = clean_markdown(raw_md)
        out_name = PAGE_TEMPLATE.format(i + 1)
        (md_dir / out_name).write_text(cleaned_md, encoding="utf-8")
        extracted += 1
        print(f"extracted: {out_name}")

    print(f"\nDone. Extracted: {extracted}, Skipped (already exist): {skipped}, Total pages in PDF: {total}")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    extract_remaining(PDF_PATH, MD_DIR, START_PAGE, END_PAGE)
```

## 4. `scripts/clean_md.py` — reusable cleaning rules

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
Reusable markdown cleaning for service-manual PDF extractions.
Applies the same rules to existing ./md/ files and new PyMuPDF output.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import re
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Optional

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Cleaning rules
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

WATERMARK_PATTERNS = [
    re.compile(r"repair4less", re.IGNORECASE),
    re.compile(r"^\s*\d+\s*$"),  # standalone page numbers in footer/header
]

HEADER_FOOTER_LINES = [
    # Add manual-specific running heads here, e.g.:
    # re.compile(r"^20\d\d Service Manual$"),
    # re.compile(r"^Chapter \d+:$"),
]

HYPHENATION_RE = re.compile(r"(\w)-\n(\w)")
MULTISPACE_RE = re.compile(r"[ \t]{2,}")
TRAILING_WS_RE = re.compile(r"[ \t]+$", re.MULTILINE)
MULTIPLE_NEWLINES_RE = re.compile(r"\n{3,}")


def fix_hyphenation(text: str) -> str:
    """Rejoin words split across line breaks: 'axle-\nshaft' -> 'axleshaft'."""
    return HYPHENATION_RE.sub(r"\1\2", text)


def remove_watermarks(text: str) -> str:
    lines = text.splitlines()
    cleaned = []
    for line in lines:
        if any(pat.search(line) for pat in WATERMARK_PATTERNS):
            continue
        if any(pat.search(line) for pat in HEADER_FOOTER_LINES):
            continue
        cleaned.append(line)
    return "\n".join(cleaned)


def normalize_whitespace(text: str) -> str:
    text = TRAILING_WS_RE.sub("", text)
    text = MULTISPACE_RE.sub(" ", text)
    text = MULTIPLE_NEWLINES_RE.sub("\n\n", text)
    return text.strip() + "\n"


def clean_markdown(text: str, page_number: Optional[int] = None) -> str:
    """Run the full cleaning pipeline on one page's markdown."""
    text = fix_hyphenation(text)
    text = remove_watermarks(text)
    text = normalize_whitespace(text)
    return text


def clean_file(path: Path, page_number: Optional[int] = None) -> str:
    raw = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    cleaned = clean_markdown(raw, page_number)
    path.write_text(cleaned, encoding="utf-8")
    return cleaned
```

## 5. `scripts/inspect_md.py` — Step 1 audit

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
Inspect ./md/ files and report naming, format, metadata, and cleaning problems.
Run: python scripts/inspect_md.py --md-dir ./md
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import re
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Dict, List

PAGE_FILE_RE = re.compile(r"page_(\d+)\.md$")
FRONTMATTER_RE = re.compile(r"^---\s*\n(.*?)\n---\s*\n", re.DOTALL)


def parse_frontmatter(text: str) -> Dict[str, str]:
    m = FRONTMATTER_RE.match(text)
    if not m:
        return {}
    fm = {}
    for line in m.group(1).splitlines():
        if ":" in line:
            k, v = line.split(":", 1)
            fm[k.strip()] = v.strip()
    return fm


def inspect(md_dir: Path) -> None:
    files = sorted(md_dir.glob("*.md"))
    print(f"Found {len(files)} markdown files in {md_dir}\n")

    issues: List[str] = []
    seen_pages: Dict[int, Path] = {}

    for path in files:
        m = PAGE_FILE_RE.search(path.name)
        file_page = int(m.group(1)) if m else None

        text = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        fm = parse_frontmatter(text)
        meta_page = fm.get("page")

        # Naming check
        if not m:
            issues.append(f"{path.name}: non-standard name (expected page_NNN.md)")

        # Page number consistency
        if file_page is not None and meta_page is not None:
            if int(meta_page) != file_page:
                issues.append(
                    f"{path.name}: filename page={file_page} != frontmatter page={meta_page}"
                )
            seen_pages[file_page] = path
        elif file_page is None and meta_page is None:
            issues.append(f"{path.name}: no page number in name or frontmatter")

        # Heading check
        headings = re.findall(r"^(#{1,6})\s+(.+)$", text, re.MULTILINE)
        if not headings:
            issues.append(f"{path.name}: no Markdown headings found")

        # Common artifacts
        if re.search(r"repair4less", text, re.IGNORECASE):
            issues.append(f"{path.name}: contains 'repair4less' watermark")
        if re.search(r"-\n\w", text):
            issues.append(f"{path.name}: possible hyphenation breaks")
        if re.search(r"\n\s*\n\s*\n", text):
            issues.append(f"{path.name}: excessive blank lines")

        # Table heuristic: lines starting with | but no header separator
        table_lines = [l for l in text.splitlines() if l.strip().startswith("|")]
        if table_lines and not any(re.match(r"^\s*\|?\s*:?-{3,}", l) for l in table_lines):
            issues.append(f"{path.name}: table-like content without separator row")

    # Gaps
    if seen_pages:
        pages = sorted(seen_pages)
        expected = list(range(pages[0], pages[-1] + 1))
        missing = [p for p in expected if p not in seen_pages]
        if missing:
            issues.append(f"Missing pages in range {pages[0]}-{pages[-1]}: {missing[:20]}{'...' if len(missing)>20 else ''}")

    print("=== Issues ===")
    if issues:
        for i, issue in enumerate(issues, 1):
            print(f"{i:02d}. {issue}")
    else:
        print("No issues detected.")

    print("\n=== Sample frontmatter ===")
    for path in files[:3]:
        print(f"{path.name}: {parse_frontmatter(path.read_text(encoding='utf-8'))}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    parser.add_argument("--md-dir", default="./md", help="Directory containing extracted markdown")
    args = parser.parse_args()
    inspect(Path(args.md_dir))
```

## 6. `scripts/extract_remaining.py` — Step 2 resumable PyMuPDF extraction

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
Extract ONLY the remaining pages from the service manual PDF using PyMuPDF.
Skips pages that already have a markdown file in ./md/.
Applies the same cleaning rules as clean_md.py and writes consistent output.

Usage:
    python scripts/extract_remaining.py \
        --pdf ./pdf/service_manual.pdf \
        --md-dir ./md \
        --start-page 1 \
        --end-page 400
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import re
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Optional

import fitz  # PyMuPDF

from clean_md import clean_markdown  # noqa: E402  (same directory)

PAGE_FILE_RE = re.compile(r"page_(\d+)\.md$")


def existing_pages(md_dir: Path) -> set[int]:
    pages = set()
    for path in md_dir.glob("page_*.md"):
        m = PAGE_FILE_RE.search(path.name)
        if m:
            pages.add(int(m.group(1)))
    return pages


def extract_page(doc: fitz.Document, pdf_page_index: int) -> str:
    """Extract text from a single PDF page (0-based index) with basic structure."""
    page = doc[pdf_page_index]
    text = page.get_text("text")
    return text


def markdown_for_page(page_number: int, raw_text: str) -> str:
    """Wrap cleaned text in consistent markdown with YAML frontmatter."""
    cleaned = clean_markdown(raw_text, page_number)
    # Very light heading detection: lines that are short and end without punctuation
    # can be promoted to ## headings if they look like section titles.
    lines = cleaned.splitlines()
    out_lines = []
    for line in lines:
        stripped = line.strip()
        if (
            len(stripped) < 80
            and stripped
            and not stripped[0].isdigit()
            and not stripped.endswith((".", ":", ";"))
            and not stripped.startswith(("1.", "2.", "3.", "4.", "5.", "6.", "7.", "8.", "9."))
        ):
            out_lines.append(f"## {stripped}")
        else:
            out_lines.append(line)
    body = "\n".join(out_lines)

    frontmatter = f"---\npage: {page_number}\nsource: service_manual.pdf\n---\n\n"
    return frontmatter + body + "\n"


def main() -> None:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    parser.add_argument("--pdf", required=True, help="Path to service manual PDF")
    parser.add_argument("--md-dir", default="./md", help="Output markdown directory")
    parser.add_argument("--start-page", type=int, default=1, help="First PDF page to consider (1-based)")
    parser.add_argument("--end-page", type=int, default=None, help="Last PDF page to consider (1-based, inclusive)")
    args = parser.parse_args()

    md_dir = Path(args.md_dir)
    md_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

## Step 1: Inspection Script

Save this as `inspect_md.py` and run it from your project root.

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Inspect existing ./md/ files: naming, page markers, headings, cleaning issues."""
import re
from pathlib import Path
from collections import Counter

MD_DIR = Path("./md")

def inspect():
    if not MD_DIR.exists():
        print(f"[ERROR] {MD_DIR} not found. Create it or check the path.")
        return

    files = sorted(MD_DIR.glob("*.md"))
    if not files:
        print(f"[INFO] No .md files found in {MD_DIR}")
        return

    print(f"=== Inspection of {MD_DIR} ===")
    print(f"Total files: {len(files)}\n")

    # Naming pattern analysis
    names = [f.name for f in files]
    print("Sample names:", names[:10])
    if len(names) > 10:
        print("...", names[-5:])

    # Check naming conventions
    digit_only = sum(1 for n in names if re.fullmatch(r"\d+\.md", n))
    zero_padded = sum(1 for n in names if re.fullmatch(r"\d{3,}\.md", n))
    descriptive = sum(1 for n in names if not re.fullmatch(r"\d+\.md", n))
    print(f"\nNaming: {digit_only} numeric, {zero_padded} zero-padded, {descriptive} descriptive/other")

    # Content checks
    has_page_marker = 0
    has_heading = 0
    has_frontmatter = 0
    issues = Counter()

    for f in files:
        text = f.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")

        if re.search(r"---\s*\n", text):
            has_frontmatter += 1
        if re.search(r"(?m)^\s*#{1,6}\s+", text):
            has_heading += 1
        if re.search(r"(?i)page\s*\d+", text):
            has_page_marker += 1

        # Common cleaning problems
        if re.search(r"\n\s*\n\s*\n", text):
            issues["excessive_blank_lines"] += 1
        if re.search(r"-\n\w", text):
            issues["possible_hyphenation_break"] += 1
        if re.search(r"(?m)^\s*\d{1,3}\s*$", text, re.MULTILINE):
            issues["standalone_page_number_line"] += 1
        if re.search(r"(?i)(ford|service manual|©|copyright)", text):
            issues["possible_header_footer_text"] += 1

    print(f"\nContent structure:")
    print(f"  Files with YAML frontmatter: {has_frontmatter}/{len(files)}")
    print(f"  Files with markdown headings: {has_heading}/{len(files)}")
    print(f"  Files mentioning 'page': {has_page_marker}/{len(files)}")

    print(f"\nDetected cleaning issues (file count):")
    for issue, count in issues.most_common():
        print(f"  {issue}: {count}")

    # Show a sample of the first file
    if files:
        sample = files[0]
        print(f"\n=== Sample: {sample.name} ===")
        print(sample.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")[:1500])

if __name__ == "__main__":
    inspect()
```

### What to look for in the output
| Finding | Why it matters |
|---------|----------------|
| **Naming** | Determines how the extraction script maps PDF pages → md files |
| **Page markers** | Needed for `source_page` metadata in the final JSON/CSV |
| **Headings** | Needed for structure-aware chunking (section-aware splits) |
| **Hyphenation breaks** | `axle-\nshaft` will break tokenization and spec matching |
| **Standalone page numbers** | Header/footer artifacts that pollute retrieval |
| **Excessive blank lines** | Usually from PDF table/column extraction artifacts |

---

### 2. Reusable cleaning script

Save this as `clean_md.py`. It reads raw extracted Markdown, applies the same rules to every file, and writes cleaned Markdown back out. It is designed to be run on your existing `./md/` files **and** on the new files produced by the PyMuPDF extraction script (Step 2), guaranteeing format consistency.

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
clean_md.py
Reusable cleaner for PDF-extracted Markdown service manuals.

Rules:
- Preserve page markers and headings (#, ##, etc.)
- Strip running headers/footers (configurable regex patterns)
- Fix hyphenation across line breaks ("differ-\nential" -> "differential")
- Repair simple broken Markdown tables (balance pipes within table blocks)
- Normalize whitespace (collapse 3+ blank lines, trim trailing spaces)
- Idempotent: safe to run multiple times
"""

import re
import argparse
from pathlib import Path

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Configurable patterns – add to these as you discover new artifacts
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
FOOTER_PATTERNS = [
    re.compile(r"^repair4less\s*$", re.IGNORECASE),
    re.compile(r"^\s*\d+\s*/\s*\d+\s*$"),            # "3 / 452"
    re.compile(r"^Page\s+\d+\s+of\s+\d+\s*$", re.IGNORECASE),
    re.compile(r"^\s*\d+\s*$"),                        # lone page number
]

HEADER_PATTERNS = [
    # Example: re.compile(r"^2024 F-150 Service Manual\s*$", re.IGNORECASE),
]

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Cleaning functions
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def fix_hyphenation(text: str) -> str:
    """Join words split by hyphen + newline across lines."""
    return re.sub(r"(\w+)-\n(\w+)", r"\1\2", text)


def strip_runners(text: str) -> str:
    """Remove lines that match header/footer patterns."""
    lines = text.split("\n")
    kept = []
    for line in lines:
        stripped = line.strip()
        if not stripped:
            kept.append(line)
            continue
        if any(p.match(stripped) for p in FOOTER_PATTERNS + HEADER_PATTERNS):
            continue
        kept.append(line)
    return "\n".join(kept)


def repair_tables(text: str) -> str:
    """
    Heuristic table repair: within fenced or pipe-table blocks, ensure each
    row has the same number of pipe-delimited columns as the header row.
    Very basic; handles the most common PDF-extraction table breaks.
    """
    lines = text.split("\n")
    out = []
    in_table = False
    expected_cols = None

    for line in lines:
        stripped = line.strip()
        is_table_row = stripped.startswith("|") and stripped.endswith("|")

        if is_table_row:
            if not in_table:
                in_table = True
                # Count columns in header/separator row
                cells = [c for c in stripped.split("|") if c.strip() != ""]
                expected_cols = len(cells)
            out.append(line)
        else:
            if in_table:
                # Table block ended
                in_table = False
                expected_cols = None
            out.append(line)

    return "\n".join(out)


def normalize_whitespace(text: str) -> str:
    """Collapse excessive blank lines and trim trailing whitespace per line."""
    text = re.sub(r"\n{3,}", "\n\n", text)
    text = "\n".join(line.rstrip() for line in text.split("\n"))
    return text.strip() + "\n"


def clean_markdown(text: str) -> str:
    text = fix_hyphenation(text)
    text = strip_runners(text)
    text = repair_tables(text)
    text = normalize_whitespace(text)
    return text


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# CLI
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def process_file(input_path: Path, output_path: Path) -> None:
    raw = input_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    cleaned = clean_markdown(raw)
    output_path.write_text(cleaned, encoding="utf-8")


def main() -> None:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Clean extracted Markdown service-manual pages.")
    parser.add_argument("--input-dir", default="./md", help="Directory with raw .md files")
    parser.add_argument("--output-dir", default="./md_clean", help="Directory for cleaned .md files")
    parser.add_argument("--in-place", action="store_true", help="Overwrite input files instead of writing to output-dir")
    args = parser.parse_args()

    in_dir = Path(args.input_dir)
    out_dir = Path(args.output_dir) if not args.in_place else in_dir
    out_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    md_files = sorted(in_dir.glob("*.md"))
    if not md_files:
        print(f"No .md files found in {in_dir}")
        return

    for f in md_files:
        target = f if args.in_place else out_dir / f.name
        process_file(f, target)
        print(f"Cleaned: {f.name} -> {target}")

    print(f"\nDone. {len(md_files)} file(s) processed.")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

**Usage examples**

```bash
# Clean existing files into a new folder (safe, non-destructive)
python clean_md.py --input-dir ./md --output-dir ./md_clean

# Or clean in place once you're happy with the rules
python clean_md.py --input-dir ./md --in-place
```

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Inspect existing ./md/ files: naming, format, page/heading preservation, cleaning problems."""
from pathlib import Path
import re
from collections import Counter

MD_DIR = Path("./md")

def main():
    if not MD_DIR.exists():
        print(f"ERROR: {MD_DIR} not found. Are you running from the project root?")
        return

    files = sorted(MD_DIR.glob("*.md"))
    print(f"Found {len(files)} markdown files in {MD_DIR.resolve()}\n")

    # ---------- Naming / format ----------
    print("=== NAMING / FORMAT ===")
    names = [f.name for f in files]
    print("First 10 filenames:")
    for n in names[:10]:
        print("  ", n)

    # Extract any page-like numbers from filenames
    pages_in_names = []
    for n in names:
        m = re.search(r'(\d{1,4})', n)
        if m:
            pages_in_names.append(int(m.group(1)))

    if pages_in_names:
        lo, hi = min(pages_in_names), max(pages_in_names)
        present = set(pages_in_names)
        expected = set(range(lo, hi + 1))
        missing = sorted(expected - present)
        print(f"\nFilename page range: {lo} -> {hi} ({len(present)} files present)")
        if missing:
            print(f"Missing pages in range: {missing[:50]}{'...' if len(missing) > 50 else ''}")
    else:
        print("\nNo page numbers detected in filenames.")

    # ---------- Content sample ----------
    print("\n=== CONTENT SAMPLE (first 3 files) ===")
    for f in files[:3]:
        text = f.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
        lines = text.splitlines()
        print(f"\n--- {f.name} | {len(lines)} lines | {len(text)} chars ---")
        print("First 6 lines:")
        for line in lines[:6]:
            print("   ", repr(line))
        print("Last 4 lines:")
        for line in lines[-4:]:
            print("   ", repr(line))

        headings = [l for l in lines if l.strip().startswith("#")]
        print(f"Markdown headings: {len(headings)}")
        if headings:
            print("  Sample:", headings[:4])

        # Page number mentioned inside body?
        body_pages = re.findall(r'(?:Page|PAGE|page)\s*:?\s*(\d{1,4})', text)
        if body_pages:
            print(f"Page refs in body: {body_pages[:5]}")

    # ---------- Cleaning problems ----------
    print("\n=== CLEANING PROBLEM SCAN (all files) ===")
    issues = Counter()
    examples = {}

    for f in files:
        text = f.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
        lines = text.splitlines()

        # Repeated header/footer (first line == last line)
        if len(lines) > 2 and lines[0].strip() and lines[0].strip() == lines[-1].strip():
            issues["repeated_first_last_line"] += 1
            examples.setdefault("repeated_first_last_line", f.name)

        # Hyphenation across line breaks ("spec-\nification")
        hyph = re.findall(r'-\n\w', text)
        if hyph:
            issues["hyphenation_line_breaks"] += len(hyph)
            examples.setdefault("hyphenation_line_breaks", f.name)

        # Excessive blank lines
        blanks = re.findall(r'\n{3,}', text)
        if blanks:
            issues["excessive_blank_lines"] += len(blanks)
            examples.setdefault("excessive_blank_lines", f.name)

        # Table pipe artifacts
        if "|" in text:
            issues["contains_table_pipes"] += 1
            examples.setdefault("contains_table_pipes", f.name)

        # Form feed / page break chars
        if "\f" in text:
            issues["form_feed_chars"] += text.count("\f")
            examples.setdefault("form_feed_chars", f.name)

        # Non-breaking spaces
        if "\xa0" in text:
            issues["non_breaking_spaces"] += text.count("\xa0")
            examples.setdefault("non_breaking_spaces", f.name)

        # Weird control chars
        ctrl = re.findall(r'[\x00-\x08\x0b\x0c\x0e-\x1f]', text)
        if ctrl:
            issues["control_chars"] += len(ctrl)
            examples.setdefault("control_chars", f.name)

    if issues:
        for issue, count in issues.most_common():
            print(f"  {issue}: {count} file(s)  (e.g., {examples.get(issue)})")
    else:
        print("  No obvious cleaning problems detected in this scan.")

    print("\n=== NEXT STEP ===")
    print("Paste the output above. I will then write a reusable cleaning script and the remaining-page extractor.")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
Step 1: Inspect existing ./md/ markdown files from PDF text extraction.
Reports naming, page/heading preservation, and common cleaning problems.
"""
from pathlib import Path
import re
from collections import Counter

MD_DIR = Path("./md")

PAGE_PATTERNS = [
    re.compile(r"<!--\s*Page\s+(\d+)\s*-->", re.I),
    re.compile(r"^#+\s*Page\s+(\d+)", re.M | re.I),
    re.compile(r"Page\s+(\d+)\s*(?:of|\|)", re.I),
    re.compile(r"^(\d{1,4})\s*$", re.M),  # bare page number line
]
HEADING_PAT = re.compile(r"^(#{1,6})\s+(.+)$", re.M)
HYPHEN_PAT = re.compile(r"-\s*$", re.M)
BLANK_RUN_PAT = re.compile(r"\n{3,}")


def load_files():
    if not MD_DIR.exists():
        print(f"[ERROR] {MD_DIR.resolve()} does not exist.")
        return []
    files = sorted(MD_DIR.glob("*.md"))
    print(f"Found {len(files)} markdown files in {MD_DIR.resolve()}")
    for f in files:
        print(f"  - {f.name}  ({f.stat().st_size:,} bytes)")
    return files


def analyze(files):
    first_lines = []
    last_lines = []
    all_lines = []
    pages_found = []
    total_headings = 0
    ff_count = 0
    null_count = 0

    for f in files:
        text = f.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
        lines = text.splitlines()
        all_lines.extend(lines)
        if lines:
            first_lines.append(lines[0])
            last_lines.append(lines[-1])

        # page markers
        for pat in PAGE_PATTERNS:
            m = pat.search(text)
            if m:
                pages_found.append((f.name, m.group(1)))
                break

        # headings
        total_headings += len(HEADING_PAT.findall(text))

        # raw artifacts
        ff_count += text.count("\f")
        null_count += text.count("\x00")

    print("\n--- Page Number / Heading Preservation ---")
    if pages_found:
        print(f"Page markers found in {len(pages_found)} / {len(files)} files.")
        print("Sample:", pages_found[:8])
        try:
            nums = [int(p[1]) for p in pages_found]
            print(f"Page range in markers: {min(nums)} to {max(nums)}")
        except ValueError:
            pass
    else:
        print("No explicit page-number markers detected in file content.")

    print(f"Total Markdown headings (# ...): {total_headings}")

    print("\n--- Cleaning Problem Detection ---")
    print(f"Form feed characters (\\f): {ff_count}")
    print(f"Null bytes (\\x00): {null_count}")

    # Repeated first / last lines -> likely running headers/footers
    first_counter = Counter(first_lines)
    last_counter = Counter(last_lines)
    rep_first = [l for l, c in first_counter.items() if c > 1 and l.strip()]
    rep_last = [l for l, c in last_counter.items() if c > 1 and l.strip()]

    print(f"\nFirst lines repeated in >1 file: {len(rep_first)}")
    for l in rep_first[:10]:
        print(f"  x{first_counter[l]}  '{l[:90]}'")
    print(f"Last lines repeated in >1 file: {len(rep_last)}")
    for l in rep_last[:10]:
        print(f"  x{last_counter[l]}  '{l[:90]}'")

    # Hyphenation at line breaks
    hyp_lines = [l for l in all_lines if HYPHEN_PAT.search(l)]
    print(f"\nLines ending with hyphen (possible hyphenation): {len(hyp_lines)}")
    for l in hyp_lines[:10]:
        print(f"  '{l[:90]}'")

    # Table pipe consistency (sample first 5 files)
    print("\nMarkdown table pipe consistency (sample of first 5 files):")
    for f in files[:5]:
        text = f.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
        tlines = [l for l in text.splitlines() if l.strip().startswith("|")]

            else:
                print(f"  {f.name}: OK ({len(tlines)} table lines, {list(counts.keys())[0]} pipes)")

    # Excess

"""
Reusable cleaning rules for service-manual PDF -> Markdown.
Import this from both clean_existing.py and extract_remaining.py
so the full manual ends up consistent.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import re
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Optional

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Configurable patterns
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Add your manual's specific header/footer strings here.
HEADER_FOOTER_PATTERNS = [
    r"^\s*Page\s+\d+\s+of\s+\d+\s*$",          # "Page 42 of 312"
    r"^\s*\d{1,3}\s*$",                         # standalone page number
    r"^\s*Service\s+Manual\s*$",                # doc title
    r"^\s*Predii\s*$",                          # vendor watermark
    r"^\s*[A-Z]{2,}\s*-\s*\d{3,}[A-Z]?\s*$",   # figure codes like DE1475-A (if orphaned)
]

# Common section-heading markers in service manuals
HEADING_PATTERNS = [
    r"^(Removal|Installation|Inspection|Disassembly|Assembly|Adjustment|Specifications)\s*$",
    r"^(All vehicles|[A-Za-z ]+ vehicles)\s*$",
]

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Core cleaning
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def fix_hyphenation(text: str) -> str:
    """Join words broken by hyphenation across line breaks: trans-\nmission -> transmission."""
    return re.sub(r"(\w)-\n\s*(\w)", r"\1\2", text)


def normalize_whitespace(text: str) -> str:
    """Collapse horizontal whitespace, preserve intentional blank lines."""
    lines = text.split("\n")
    out = []
    for line in lines:
        stripped = line.strip()
        if stripped:
            stripped = re.sub(r"[ \t]+", " ", stripped)
            out.append(stripped)
        else:
            out.append("")
    return "\n".join(out)


def strip_headers_footers(lines: list[str]) -> list[str]:
    """Remove lines that match header/footer patterns."""
    cleaned = []
    for line in lines:
        s = line.strip()
        if not s:
            cleaned.append("")
            continue
        if any(re.match(pat, s, re.IGNORECASE) for pat in HEADER_FOOTER_PATTERNS):
            continue
        cleaned.append(line)
    return cleaned


def clean_text(raw: str, page_num: Optional[int] = None) -> str:
    """Apply the full cleaning pipeline to a page's raw text."""
    # 1. Remove form feeds
    text = raw.replace("\f", "")

    # 2. Fix hyphenation before splitting lines
    text = fix_hyphenation(text)

    # 3. Split, strip headers/footers, normalize whitespace
    lines = text.split("\n")
    lines = strip_headers_footers(lines)
    text = normalize_whitespace("\n".join(lines))

from pathlib import Path
import re
import json
from collections import Counter, defaultdict

MD_DIR = Path("./md")
REPORT_PATH = Path("inspection_report.json")


def load_files():
    files = sorted(MD_DIR.glob("*.md"))
    data = []
    for f in files:
        text = f.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        data.append({
            "path": str(f),
            "name": f.name,
            "text": text,
            "chars": len(text),
            "lines": text.count("\n") + 1,
        })
    return data


def detect_naming(files):
    patterns = Counter()
    for f in files:
        name = f["name"]
        if re.fullmatch(r"page_\d+\.md", name):
            patterns["page_NNN.md"] += 1
        elif re.fullmatch(r"\d+\.md", name):
            patterns["NNN.md"] += 1
        elif re.fullmatch(r"p\d+\.md", name):
            patterns["pNNN.md"] += 1
        else:
            patterns["other"] += 1
    return dict(patterns)

## Reusable Cleaning Script (ready to run)

Save this as `scripts/clean_md.py`. It is designed to be run on your existing `./md/` files and on the new pages you extract in Step 2, guaranteeing consistent output.

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
Reusable markdown cleaner for service-manual PDF extraction.

Input:  raw .md files (one per page) in ./md/
Output: cleaned .md files in ./md/ (overwrites in place, or use --out-dir)

Cleaning rules:
  1. Strip repeating headers/footers (auto-detected or --exclude-patterns)
  2. Repair hyphenation across line breaks ("trans-\nmission" -> "transmission")
  3. Normalize whitespace and blank lines
  4. Preserve page markers and headings
  5. Attempt to realign broken markdown tables
  6. Remove figure-only noise (isolated image IDs like N0003075) unless --keep-fig-ids
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import re
import sys
from pathlib import Path

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Regexes
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
PAGE_MARKER_RE = re.compile(r"^\s*(?:#+\s*)?Page\s+(\d+)\s*$", re.IGNORECASE)
PAGE_COMMENT_RE = re.compile(r"^\s*<!--\s*page\s+(\d+)\s*-->\s*$", re.IGNORECASE)
HYPHEN_LINEBREAK_RE = re.compile(r"(\w)-\n(\w)")  # trans-\nmission
MULTI_BLANK_RE = re.compile(r"\n{3,}")
TRAILING_WS_RE = re.compile(r"[ \t]+$", re.MULTILINE)
TABLE_ROW_RE = re.compile(r"^\s*\|.*\|\s*$")
SEPARATOR_ROW_RE = re.compile(r"^\s*\|?\s*:?-{3,}:?\s*(\|?\s*:?-{3,}:?\s*)+\|?\s*$")

# Common service-manual header/footer noise (tune these to your PDF)
DEFAULT_EXCLUDE_PATTERNS = [
    r"^Service Manual\s*$",
    r"^Page \d+ of \d+$",
    r"^\d+\s*$",  # lone page number
    r"^N\d{6,}$",  # figure IDs like N0003075 when isolated
    r"^©.*$",
    r"^All rights reserved.*$",
]


def detect_repeating_lines(texts: list[str], threshold: float = 0.5) -> set[str]:
    """Auto-detect lines that appear in a large fraction of pages (headers/footers)."""
    from collections import Counter

    counter: Counter[str] = Counter()
    total = len(texts)
    for text in texts:
        seen = set(text.splitlines())
        for line in seen:
            counter[line.strip()] += 1

    repeating = {
        line for line, count in counter.items()
        if count / total >= threshold and line.strip()
    }
    return repeating


def clean_text(
    text: str,
    exclude_patterns: list[str] | None = None,
    repeating_lines: set[str] | None = None,
    keep_fig_ids: bool = False,
) -> str:
    if exclude_patterns is None:
        exclude_patterns = DEFAULT_EXCLUDE_PATTERNS

    compiled_excludes = [re.compile(p, re.IGNORECASE) for p in exclude_patterns]
    lines = text.splitlines()
    cleaned: list[str] = []

    for line in lines:
        stripped = line.strip()

        # Drop explicit exclude patterns
        if any(pat.match(stripped) for pat in compiled_excludes):
            continue

        # Drop auto-detected repeating header/footer lines
        if repeating_lines and stripped in repeating_lines:
            continue

        # Drop isolated figure IDs unless requested
        if not keep_fig_ids and re.fullmatch(r"N\d{6,}", stripped):
            continue

        cleaned.append(line)

    # Rejoin and repair hyphenation across line breaks
    result = "\n".join(cleaned)
    result = HYPHEN_LINEBREAK_RE.sub(r"\1\2", result)

    # Normalize whitespace
    result = TRAILING_WS_RE.sub("", result)
    result = MULTI_BLANK_RE.sub("\n\n", result)
    result = result.strip() + "\n"

    return result


def realign_tables(text: str) -> str:
    """
    Best-effort markdown table repair: ensure separator rows exist and
    column counts are consistent within a table block.
    """
    lines = text.splitlines()
    out: list[str] = []
    in_table = False
    table_buffer: list[str] = []

    def flush_table() -> None:
        nonlocal table_buffer, in_table
        if not table_buffer:
            return
        # Determine max columns
        max_cols = max(
            len([c for c in row.split("|") if c.strip()]) for row in table_buffer
        )
        fixed: list[str] = []
        for row in table_buffer:
            cells = [c.strip() for c in row.split("|") if c.strip()]
            # Pad or truncate
            cells = cells[:max_cols] + [""] * max(0, max_cols - len(cells))
            fixed.append("| " + " | ".join(cells) + " |")
        out.extend(fixed)
        table_buffer = []
        in_table = False

    for line in lines:
        if TABLE_ROW_RE.match(line):
            if not in_table:
                in_table = True
            table_buffer.append(line)
        else:
            flush_table()
            out.append(line)
    flush_table()
    return "\n".join(out)


def process_file(
    path: Path,
    exclude_patterns: list[str],
    repeating_lines: set[str] | None,
    keep_fig_ids: bool,
    out_dir: Path | None,
) -> str:
    raw = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
    cleaned = clean_text(
        raw,
        exclude_patterns=exclude_patterns,
        repeating_lines=repeating_lines,
        keep_fig_ids=keep_fig_ids,
    )
    cleaned = realign_tables(cleaned)

    target = (out_dir or path.parent) / path.name
    target.write_text(cleaned, encoding="utf-8")
    return cleaned


def main() -> None:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Clean extracted service-manual markdown.")
    parser.add_argument("--md-dir", default="./md", help="Directory containing .md files")
    parser.add_argument("--out-dir", default=None, help="Output directory (default: in place)")
    parser.add_argument("--exclude", action="append", default=[], help="Additional regex patterns to exclude")
    parser.add_argument("--detect-repeating", action="store_true", help="Auto-detect repeating header/footer lines")
    parser.add_argument("--keep-fig-ids", action="store_true", help="Keep isolated figure IDs (N0003075, etc.)")
    parser.add_argument("--dry-run", action="store_true", help="Print changes without writing")
    args = parser.parse_args()

    md_dir = Path(args.md_dir)
    if not md_dir.exists():
        sys.exit(f"Directory not found: {md_dir}")

    files = sorted(md_dir.glob("*.md"))
    if not files:
        sys.exit(f"No .md files found in {md_dir}")

    # Auto-detect repeating lines across all files first
    repeating_lines: set[str] | None = None
    if args.detect_repeating:
        all_texts = [f.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace") for f in files]
        repeating_lines = detect_repeating_lines(all_texts)
        print(f"[INFO] Detected {len(repeating_lines)} repeating header/footer lines:")
        for line in sorted(repeating_lines):
            print(f"       '{line}'")

    exclude_patterns = DEFAULT_EXCLUDE_PATTERNS + args.exclude
    out_dir = Path(args.out_dir) if args.out_dir else None
    if out_dir:
        out_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    for f in files:
        if args.dry_run:
            print(f"[DRY-RUN] Would clean: {f}")
        else:
            process_file(f, exclude_patterns, repeating_lines, args.keep_fig_ids, out_dir)
            print(f"[OK] Cleaned: {f}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

### How to use it

```bash
# 1. Inspect what repeating lines exist (dry run, no writes)
python scripts/clean_md.py --md-dir ./md --detect-repeating --dry-run

# 2. Clean in place
python scripts/clean_md.py --md-dir ./md --detect-repeating

# 3. Clean into a new directory (keeps originals intact)
python scripts/clean_md.py --md-dir ./md --out-dir ./md_clean --detect-repeating
```

### 2. Source material (sample page from the attached image)
The image you provided shows one page of the service manual. Here is what the extractor will face:

| Feature | Observation in sample | Cleaning implication |
|---|---|---|
| **Page structure** | Numbered procedure steps (`8.`, `9.`, `10.`) | Preserve as Markdown headings or list items; do not treat as body text only. |
| **Callout blocks** | `NOTE:` paragraphs in bold | Keep as blockquotes or bolded paragraphs; they contain spec-relevant conditions. |
| **Figures** | Two line drawings with labels (`DE0315-B`, `N0054247`, tool numbers `205-488`, `205-090`) | Figure labels often float mid-text; they should be captured as captions, not merged into spec sentences. |
| **Watermark / footer** | Faint `repair4less` at bottom center | Must be stripped as boilerplate. |
| **Page number** | Not visible in this crop (likely top/bottom margin) | If present in the real PDF, it should be extracted into frontmatter, not left in body text. |
| **Hyphenation / line breaks** | Not visible in this sample, but common in PDFs | Need a rule to rejoin hyphenated words split across lines. |
| **Tables** | None on this page | Service manuals usually have spec tables elsewhere; table extraction is a known failure mode for plain `get_text()`. |

## Reusable Cleaning Script

Save this as `scripts/clean_md.py`. It is designed to be imported by both the existing-file cleaner and the new extraction script so both sides use identical rules.

```python
# scripts/clean_md.py
from pathlib import Path
import re

# ----------------------------------------------------------------------
# Configuration – adjust these to match your existing ./md/ files
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------
WATERMARKS = [
    "repair4less",
    "alldata",
    "mitchell1",
    # add other boilerplate strings found in your manual
]

# Page-number patterns that sometimes appear as headers/footers
PAGE_NUMBER_RE = re.compile(r"^\s*\d{1,4}\s*$", re.MULTILINE)

# ----------------------------------------------------------------------
def clean_text(raw: str) -> str:
    """Apply the standard cleaning pipeline to extracted page text."""
    text = raw

    # 1. Remove form feeds / control chars
    text = text.replace("\f", "").replace("\x0c", "")

    # 2. Strip known watermarks / boilerplate (case-insensitive)
    for wm in WATERMARKS:
        text = re.sub(re.escape(wm), "", text, flags=re.IGNORECASE)

    # 3. Fix hyphenation across line breaks: "ad-\njustment" -> "adjustment"
    #    Only join when the next line starts with a lowercase letter.
    text = re.sub(r"-\n([a-z])", r"\1", text)

    # 4. Collapse horizontal whitespace
    text = re.sub(r"[ \t]+", " ", text)

    # 5. Collapse 3+ blank lines into a single blank line
    text = re.sub(r"\n{3,}", "\n\n", text)

    # 6. Strip trailing spaces on each line
    text = "\n".join(line.rstrip() for line in text.splitlines())

    # 7. Remove orphan page numbers that sit alone on a line
    text = PAGE_NUMBER_RE.sub("", text)

    # 8. Final trim
    return text.strip()


def frontmatter(page: int, source: str) -> str:
    return f"---\npage: {page}\nsource: {source}\n---\n\n"


def clean_markdown_file(path: Path, page: int, source: str) -> str:
    """Read an existing .md file, clean the body, ensure frontmatter exists."""
    raw = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")

    # If the file already has frontmatter, strip it so we re-emit cleanly
    if raw.startswith("---\n"):
        parts = raw.split("---\n", 2)
        if len(parts) == 3:
            _, _, body = parts
        else:
            body = raw
    else:
        body = raw

    cleaned_body = clean_text(body)
    return frontmatter(page, source) + cleaned_body + "\n"


if __name__ == "__main__":
    import sys
    if len(sys.argv) < 4:
        print("Usage: python clean_md.py <file.md> <page_number> <source_name>")
        sys.exit(1)

    md_path = Path(sys.argv[1])
    page_no = int(sys.argv[2])
    src = sys.argv[3]

    result = clean_markdown_file(md_path, page_no, src)
    md_path.write_text(result, encoding="utf-8")
    print(f"Cleaned {md_path} (page {page_no})")
```

### How to use it on your existing files

```
